# Rulings

**Status** · Every ruling, one line each (PROCESS-MECHANICS §9; P15): `K<n> · date · module or layer · the ruling · why`. A ruling is changed only by a new line naming its evidence and the one it replaces. Where a ruling has a fuller home, the line names it. Started by BOB #38, 2026-09-26, from the rulings made during the transition.

- K1 · 2026-09-25 · all · No jurisdiction in the product: local facts are data in jurisdiction profiles held by `jurisdictions`; outward text names CivicOS and the group · Bob's concern that Oakland references tell other groups CivicOS is not for them (`layers.md`, "No jurisdiction in the product")
- K2 · 2026-09-25 · all · No module size limit; size is a watched metric, reported near 4,000 lines · Bob (`layers.md`, rulings on the draft, 1)

- K156 · 2026-09-27 · process (P3, K113; civicos-process mechanics §2, §5, §6, §13, `roles/ROOT.md`) · The K154 revision: a ROOT session that Bob starts, at depth 0, is the parent of every BOB; a BOB that refreshes pushes its handoff and asks ROOT by one-time routine (`START BOB`) to start its successor, so every BOB sits at depth 1 and every job at depth 2, whatever the number of handovers. ROOT does nothing else and is replaced only by Bob (context past 300k tokens, or failed; the BOB's handoff then says `NEEDS BOB: start ROOT`). Every BOB reads its depth by following `parent_session_id` and writes it in the Status line; the 20-minute backstop reads each job's record as well as its session state, and a message pushed in a record is handled as if it had arrived. Routine-started sessions carry no connector, session or routine tools and are never ROOT, a BOB or a job. Certified by dry run D7, 2026-09-27 ~14:12 UTC (civicos-process `dryrun/CERTIFICATION.md` L1–L7, `RUNS.md` D7: ROOT started a sibling successor at depth 1, a job at depth 2 messaged its BOB by routine; 3.78M tokens) · Bob's design, 2026-09-27 (in BOB #47's session), replacing BOB #47's draft of a bounded chain (a BOB starting its successor only at depth 5 or less, Bob restarting it every seven BOBs); measured: the chain BOB #38 → #40 → … → #46 put BOB #46 at depth 7 and its jobs at 8.

- K168 · 2026-09-27 · process (K164; P3, P13, P16, P17) · Bob approves BOB #49's architectural simplification of the K164 revision: communication between sessions is **mailbox plus doorbell**. Each message is a numbered entry in its writer's own file in git (a job's record; BOB's outbox for that job on the tranche branch); each side keeps a cursor, the last entry it processed; a routine carries no content, only "check your mailbox", so a late, duplicated, reordered or lost routine cannot corrupt state, and BOB's backstop rings again for any side whose cursor is behind. Liveness is judged from git, never from platform status. A small tool, `tools/mail.mjs` in civicos-process, writes and reads the mailboxes so no session hand-writes protocol (R2's one failure). Kept from K164: ROOT, the WATCH dead-man (K167), the two-step close. Cut: the envelope grammar inside routines, the plan-line bookkeeping kept only for grading, and the 71-scenario suite as a standing requirement (P3 asks a small dry run). Certified by one small dry run; T7 then opens with its smallest layer first · Bob, 2026-09-27: "Simplicity is elegance."
